Image-center middle ground shows the husband seated facing image-left; torso square to picture plane, pelvis tilted toward her, both feet planted on floor planks, right hand reaching to touch her exposed moon-white silk under-robe at the collar, left hand gripping the couch rail behind him. The palette families are warm-tea silk ground, soot-ink line anchor, autumn-fragrance yellow and ochre warm tones, muted azurite cool accent, sandalwood brown and lotus-root pink textile neutrals, restrained shell-white skin base; contour-enclosed color fields with exactly three bounded skin tones—shell-white base, transparent warm flesh wash at cheeks and palms, narrow deeper line-wash beneath jaw and where the kneeling woman's face meets abdomen; gossamer faces, iron-wire furniture, layered mineral washes. Uneven warm-tea field extends full-bleed to every canvas edge; broken tea-brown edge-oxidation dissolves inward from lower left side; rubbed silk fibers at upper right corner; one shallow age crease crosses blank workshop wall; three localized changes: azurite fan frame details powder slightly at exposed edges, rouge cheek wash on the standing woman remains stronger in protected profile recess, a narrow band of softened ink contour runs along the table's image-left front edge where handling polish accumulated. The entire image remains a visibly aged, flat, hand-painted gongbi silk album leaf made from ink contours and mineral pigment, with individualized mature adult faces defined by fine line.

The palette: warm-ivory prepared-silk ground, soot ink and charred ink for architecture and text grids, persimmon and cinnabar in the opened jacket and flushed cheeks, sandalwood brown in the hiked skirt and brush rests, muted blue-green in the displaced scholar's cap and examination jacket, lotus-root pink in the fallen under-robe linings, with restrained shell-white highlighting the women's collar edges and the bun's gold-wash accents; contour-enclosed color fields with exactly three bounded skin tones of pale ochre base, transparent warm flesh wash on cheeks and contact palms, narrow deeper line-wash beneath the angular woman's jaw and where the younger woman's forearm presses his shoulder. The uneven warm-tea silk field reaches every canvas edge; broken asymmetrical edge-oxidation patch dissolves inward from lower right; rubbed upper left corner with visible silk warp; one shallow age crease crosses blank silk above the desk; three localized changes: azurite in the scholar's cap showing powdering at its exposed crown, softened outer ink contour on the inkstone's far edge, faint handling polish on the papers' corner where fingers have turned them. The entire image remains a visibly aged, flat, hand-painted gongbi silk album leaf made from ink contours and mineral pigment, with individualized mature adult faces defined by fine line.
